# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-09-20 through 2026-09-26  
**Updated:** 2026-09-20T10:43:07+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-01-18

## Best date plans

### 2026-09-20 — FAD Market: Open Streets on Montague + Haenyeo

**FAD Market: Open Streets on Montague, then a meal.** Korean at Haenyeo. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Montague+Street+between+Clinton-Henry+and+Henry-Hicks+Streets%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=239+5th+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://fadmarket.co/open-streets-on-montague) · [Restaurant](https://www.haenyeobk.com/)

### 2026-09-22 — Theodora + The Monkathon: Miles Okazaki plays Thelonious Monk

**Dinner, then The Monkathon: Miles Okazaki plays Thelonious Monk.** Mediterranean, seafood, wood-fired at Theodora. It is a 7-minute walk from the venue. Doors 7 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=7+Greene+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&destination=509+Atlantic+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://roulette.org/event/the-monkathon-film-screening-miles-okazaki/) · [Event booking](https://ci.ovationtix.com/36368/production/1283526) · [Restaurant](https://www.theodorabk.com/)

### 2026-09-20 — Washington Square Park Folk Festival + Noreetuh

**Washington Square Park Folk Festival, then a meal.** Hawaiian, New American at Noreetuh. It is a 13-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 1 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Washington+Square+Park%3A+Garibaldi+Plaza&destination=128+1st+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.wspfolkfest.com) · [Restaurant](https://www.noreetuh.com/)

### 2026-09-24 — Claro + The Scores Project: A Book Release Show with The Rise of the Novel

**Dinner, then The Scores Project: A Book Release Show with The Rise of the Novel.** Oaxacan Mexican at Claro. It is a 14-minute walk from the venue. Doors 7 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=284+3rd+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=509+Atlantic+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://roulette.org/event/the-scores-project-the-rise-of-the-novel/) · [Event booking](https://ci.ovationtix.com/36368/production/1281983?performanceId=11836541) · [Restaurant](https://www.clarobk.com/)

### 2026-09-21 — Buck Wild Line Dancing at Domino Square + Nura

**Buck Wild Line Dancing at Domino Square, then dinner.** New American, Indian-influenced, cocktails at Nura. It is a 19-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=15+River+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&destination=46+Norman+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11222&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.dominopark.com/events/buck-wild-line-dancing-2) · [Restaurant](https://www.nurabk.com/)

### 2026-09-20 — Haenyeo + Jinpa

**Dinner, then Jinpa.** Korean at Haenyeo. It is a 14-minute walk from the venue. Jinpa starts at 7:15 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=239+5th+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=30+Lafayette+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.bam.org/film/2026/a-pure-person-jinpa) · [Restaurant](https://www.haenyeobk.com/)

### 2026-09-26 — Nura + Erik Hall: Music for 18 Musicians & Canto Ostinato

**Dinner, then Erik Hall: Music for 18 Musicians & Canto Ostinato.** New American, Indian-influenced, cocktails at Nura. It is a 14-minute walk from the venue. Erik Hall: Music for 18 Musicians & Canto Ostinato starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=46+Norman+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11222&destination=80+N+6th+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nationalsawdust.org/event/erik-hall-music-for-18-musicians-canto-ostinato-performance-spatial-audio-installation) · [Event booking](https://ci.ovationtix.com/36490/production/1280437) · [Restaurant](https://www.nurabk.com/)

### 2026-09-20 — House of Yes Block Party + Win Son

**House of Yes Block Party, then a meal.** Taiwanese American at Win Son. It is a 21-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=2+Wyckoff+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11237&destination=159+Graham+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11206&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://calendar.houseofyes.org/e/jbGeEKPbX5) · [Restaurant](https://winsonbrooklyn.com/)

#### [Queens Night Market](https://queensnightmarket.com/)

4:00 PM · markets · Free entry; most dishes capped at a few dollars · Queens Night Market · Corona · Queens · Score 56.0  
Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.
**Timing:** Saturdays 4pm to midnight. Note the seasonal gap between late August and mid September.
**Practical note:** Outdoors and cash-friendly; the adjacent lot charges for parking, so the 7 train is easier.
**Why now:** Runs every Saturday evening in season; the 7 train to 111th St drops you four blocks away.

## Where to eat

Verified Sept. 20 dining picks against captured public evidence and limited follow-up pages; kept useful current restaurants, deals and dated events while downgrading older heatmap openings and ordinary menus where needed.

Dining research: 2026-09-20T10:31:07+00:00

No NYC candidate was retained as Michelin-starred. Eater states that the Mexico City original El Califa de León became the world’s first Michelin-starred taqueria in 2024; the NYC Flatiron location is treated only as an American debut, not as a starred NYC restaurant. No unsourced award claims were added.

### Elder opened as a British Indian chophouse in Hudson Yards

New openings — Elder

Eater's September Manhattan heatmap added chef Sujan Sarkar's Elder, describing steaks with Tellicherry peppercorns, burrah-spiced lamb chops, and a vegetable-forward selection.

Conditions: Eater verified Elder at 517 West 38th Street and says it opened in August 2026; official site text was not captured, so keep menu/hours unverified. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#elder  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#elder)

### Cote 550 is Cote’s Midtown Korean steakhouse expansion

More restaurants — Cote 550

Eater lists Cote 550 as Simon Kim's second Manhattan Cote location within the 550 Madison project, with a more upscale Butcher's Feast and steak omakase angle.

Conditions: Eater says Cote 550 opened in April 2026, so it should not be framed as a new September opening; keep as a restaurant listing. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#cote-550  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#cote-550)

### Cospa Ramen serves late-night Flatiron ramen

New openings — Cospa Ramen

Eater's September Manhattan update added Cospa Ramen as a single-focused ramen spot; the page image caption specifically highlights spicy ramen.

Conditions: Eater gives the address as 36 West 26th Street, says Cospa opened in August, starts ramen around $10 with the spicy ramen about $1 more, offers walk-in seating and QR-code ordering, and is open 10:30am-2am; Eater also says it has no website. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#cospa-ramen  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#cospa-ramen)

### El Califa de León debuts in Flatiron

New openings — El Califa de León

Eater's Manhattan September update added the American debut of the Mexico City taqueria El Califa de León.

Conditions: Eater gives the NYC address as 20 West 23rd Street and says the Flatiron location landed in August 2026; do not imply the NYC location has a Michelin star. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#el-califa-de-leon-ny  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#el-califa-de-leon-ny)

### Mary’s Fish Camp returns on Hudson Street

New openings — Mary's Fish Camp

Eater's September Manhattan update added the return of Mary’s Fish Camp, useful for classic seafood in the West Village.

Conditions: Eater says Mary’s Fish Camp returned in late July at 626 Hudson Street after the original Charles Street location closed in 2024; walk-ins and Resy reservations are mentioned, but official menu/hours were not captured. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#marys-fish-camp  
Published: 2026-09-03  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#marys-fish-camp)

### Hommali brings contemporary Thai to the West Village

New openings — Hommali

Eater's September Manhattan update added Hommali, described as a fun contemporary Thai restaurant in the West Village.

Conditions: Eater says Hommali opened in September at 28 7th Avenue South; keep dish examples Eater names, but official menu/hours were not captured. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap#hommali

#### [Much Ado About Nothing](https://muchadobroadway.com/)

2026-10-31 · First preview October 31, 2026; opens November 19, 2026; closes January 10, 2027. Runtime 2 hours 20 minutes with one intermission. · theater · Ticket prices vary · Winter Garden Theatre · Theater District · Manhattan · Score 54.0  
Tom Hiddleston and Hayley Atwell star in Jamie Lloyd's Broadway production of Shakespeare's Much Ado About Nothing at the Winter Garden Theatre.
**Practical note:** Limited Broadway run with a fixed January 10 closing date; buy through the official Broadway.org/Telecharge path.
**Why now:** The official Broadway League page confirms first preview, opening, and closing dates within the book-ahead horizon.
**On-sale watch:** Broadway.org lists a January 10, 2027 closing date for this star-led limited Broadway run, so the run cannot be treated as open-ended.

## Discovery coverage

_No API key is needed for these public websites. `not checked` is distinct from a checked source with no final pick._

- **[arts] 92NY — checked, no retained pick:** Sampled in research pass, but official page output did not expose usable dated listings for retention.
- **[arts] Americas Society — not checked:** Optional source not checked.
- **[arts] Anthology Film Archives — used:** Official September 2026 calendar consulted; retained four screenings from September 20-22.
- **[arts] Asia Society New York — not checked:** Optional source not checked.
- **[arts] Atlantic Theater Company — not checked:** Optional source not checked.
- **[arts] Balance — used:** largest source: Metrograph 7/42; Brooklyn 9/42.
